Method: 1. 在表示层面：通过正交投影模块去除融合图文表示中的主导单模态方向，保留语用残差；同时使用一个表面情感分类器为解码器提供极性差异标签。
2. 在输出层面：模型生成结构化推理链以显式表达理解过程。
3. 在目标层面：引入对比奖励机制，惩罚仅复述字面描述的答案。

Result: 在六个多模态基准测试中，Intent Projection持续优于开源基线模型，并缩小了与专有模型之间的差距，尤其在字面与意图差异较大的帖子上提升最显著。

Conclusion: 通过在单一LVLM架构内实现字面-语用分解，Intent Projection有效缓解了‘字面坍缩’问题，提升了模型对讽刺性和隐含意义内容的理解能力，为社交媒体内容理解提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: When asked what a meme or sarcastic post means, Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) tend to describe what the image shows rather than what the author is trying to communicate. Standard instruction tuning entangles a post's literal content with its pragmatic meaning, letting surface-level details contaminate the final response. We reframe meme understanding as a problem of literal-pragmatic decomposition and propose \textbf{Intent Projection}, a framework that separates the two signals at the representation, output, and objective levels within a single LVLM backbone. At the representation level, an orthogonal projection module removes dominant unimodal directions from the fused image-text representation, retaining only the pragmatic residual, while a surface-real affect classifier anchors the decoder with a discrete tag that names the polarity gap. At the output level, the model externalizes a structured reasoning chain, and at the objective level a contrastive reward explicitly penalizes answers that restate the literal description. Across six multimodal benchmarks, Intent Projection consistently outperforms open-source baselines and narrows the gap to proprietary models, with the largest gains on high-divergence posts where literal collapse is most damaging.

</details>


### [188] [Building Reliable Long-Form Generation via Hallucination Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03628)
*Lin Li,Georgia Channing,Suhaas M Bhat,Gabriel Davis Jones,Yarin Gal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的推理时幻觉缓解框架SHARS，通过分段检测和拒绝幻觉内容来减少长文本生成中的错误累积，提升事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长文本生成中容易产生幻觉，且早期错误会不断累积加剧问题，影响可靠性。

Method: 提出了分段幻觉拒绝采样（SHARS）框架，利用语义不确定性作为检测器，在生成过程中识别并拒绝幻觉片段，并重新采样以生成忠实内容。

Result: 在标准幻觉基准测试中显著减少了长文本生成中的幻觉现象，同时保持甚至提升了生成内容的信息量。

Conclusion: SHARS能有效缓解大语言模型在长文本生成中的幻觉累积问题，提升生成结果的事实准确性与可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in open-ended text generation, yet they remain prone to hallucinating incorrect or unsupported content, which undermines their reliability. This issue is exacerbated in long-form generation due to hallucination snowballing, a phenomenon where early errors propagate and compound into subsequent outputs. To address this challenge, we propose a novel inference-time hallucination mitigation framework, named Segment-wise HAllucination Rejection Sampling (SHARS), which uses an arbitrary hallucination detector to identify and reject hallucinated segments during generation and resample until faithful content is produced. By retaining only confident information and building subsequent generations upon it, the framework mitigates hallucination accumulation and enhances factual consistency. To instantiate this framework, we adopt semantic uncertainty as the detector and introduce several vital modifications to address its limitations and better adapt it to long-form text. Our method enables models to self-correct hallucinations without requiring external resources such as web search or knowledge bases, while remaining compatible with them for future extensions. Empirical evaluations on standardized hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that our method substantially reduces hallucinations in long-form generation while preserving or even improving the informativeness of generation. Code is available at: https://github.com/TreeLLi/hallucination-rejection-sampling.

</details>


### [189] [Safety Measurements for Fine-tuned LLMs Should be Grounded in Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03648)
*Krishnapriya Vishnubhotla,Hillary Dawkins,Isar Nejadgholi,Svetlana Kiritchenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在特定能力目标下微调大语言模型对安全性的影响，发现微调可能导致模型在安全提示下生成不连贯内容，自动化安全评估对此类输出不可靠，且安全评估结果受基准和评估者选择影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在微调对模型安全性影响方面缺乏系统性实验设计，本文旨在通过锚定具体能力目标来建立一致的评估框架。

Method: 通过多维度评估方法，聚焦微调对模型能力和安全性的共同影响，比较不同安全基准和评估者的判断差异。

Result: 发现微调后的模型可能对安全提示生成不连贯内容；自动化安全判断对这些内容不可靠；安全评估结论随基准和评估者变化而变化。

Conclusion: 为准确评估微调对安全性的影响，需明确能力目标，并谨慎选择安全基准与评估方式，避免得出片面或错误结论。

Abstract: Adapting foundation large language models to a user's task or preferred style through fine-tuning can result in compromising the model's safety. Previous works examined the effects of fine-tuning on model safety in limited and seemingly random experimental settings. We argue that anchoring fine-tuning to a specific capability goal is essential for avoiding arbitrary empirical choices, allowing us to draw meaningful conclusions about safety impacts, and to compare mitigation methods on a consistent basis. We conduct a multi-dimensional evaluation of the effects of fine-tuning on model behavior by focusing on capability as well as safety. Our results surface important issues that (1) fine-tuned models can produce incoherent generations in response to safety prompts, (2) automated safety judgments are unreliable for such incoherent outputs, and (3) the conclusions about the effects of fine-tuning can change depending on the choice of safety benchmark as well as the safety evaluator.

</details>


### [190] [CoEval: Ranking Language Models for Custom Tasks Without Labeled Data or Trustworthy Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03650)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoEval是一个开源、可重用的框架，能够在无标注数据和避免基准污染的情况下，通过教师模型生成任务相关的基准测试，并利用跨厂商的裁判模型集成对候选语言模型进行排序，实现了无需人工标注和防污染的自动化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏特定任务标注数据且公共基准可能存在预训练数据泄露的情况下，传统模型选择方法容易受到记忆化而非实际性能的影响，因此需要一种无需人工标注、防止数据污染且可复用的模型评估方案。

Method: 提出CoEval框架：1）根据任务描述由教师模型自动生成可控属性的新测试样本，确保每次运行都无数据重复；2）采用跨厂商、多模型组成的裁判集成系统对候选模型输出进行打分排序，避免单一裁判偏差和同源偏好；3）整个流程无需人类参与标注或评分。

Result: 实验验证表明CoEval能准确恢复真实模型排名，与真实标签的相关性达ho=0.86；生成的测试项与五大公共基准无任何13-gram重叠；小规模但多样化的裁判组合最可靠，避免了冗长偏见和自偏好；四任务共7978次评估仅耗资5.89美元。

Conclusion: CoEval提供了一种低成本、可重复、防污染且无需人工干预的模型评估解决方案，适用于任意领域，可随新模型发布重新运行，使各团队能为自身应用场景构建专属的可信排行榜。

Abstract: Choosing or ranking language models for a specific application is hardest when no task-specific labeled data exists, and standard public benchmarks cannot be trusted, their items having likely leaked into pretraining, so scores reflect memorization rather than fitness. We present CoEval, an open-source, reusable framework that closes this gap end to end: from only a description of a task or domain, teacher models synthesize a fresh, attribute-controlled benchmark with no human labels, contamination-free because items are generated anew on each run, and a cross-family judge ensemble ranks candidate models with no human raters. Validated where ground truth exists, CoEval recovers the true model ranking and tracks ground-truth correctness at ho=0.86. The label-free judging needs no human calibration because judge-panel composition (vendor diversity), not size, drives reliability: a small, well-chosen cross-family panel is most reliable, while a single judge can be anti-correlated with ground truth (judge-choice regret 0.35) and the ensemble never is. Generated items show zero verbatim 13-gram overlap with five major public benchmarks; the panel cancels verbosity bias and precludes same-family self-preference. A four-task study produced 7,978 evaluations for USD 5.89. The same declarative pipeline applies to any domain and is cheap enough to re-run on every model release: a label-free, contamination-free leaderboard any team can regenerate for its own application.

</details>


### [191] [Does Language Shift Break Medical Vision-Language Models? Indonesian Radiology Visual Question Answering Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03693)
*Pieter Christy Yan Yudhistira,Dzaki Rafif Malik,Novanto Yudistira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IndoRad-VQA，这是VQA-RAD的印尼语版本，用于评估医学视觉语言模型在使用印尼语提问时的放射学推理能力。研究发现，尽管模型在英语医学VQA基准测试中表现良好，但在印尼语临床环境中的鲁棒性显著下降，英语和印尼语设置之间的性能差距为8%到25%。结果强调了对多语言医学多模态基础模型进行更包容性评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学视觉语言模型（VLMs）主要在英文放射科视觉问答基准上进行评估，其在非英语临床语言环境下的鲁棒性尚不明确。因此，需要一个非英语的评估基准来检验这些模型在其他语言（如印尼语）中的实际表现。

Method: 构建了一个名为IndoRad-VQA的印尼语医学视觉问答数据集，作为VQA-RAD的印尼语适配版本。通过基于自我评估的质量控制机制将原问题-答案对翻译成印尼语，确保临床含义、术语一致性和答案等效性。在英语和印尼语提示下，评估了通用型、东南亚多语言型及医学专用型VLM的表现，并量化了两种语言输入之间的性能差异。此外还进行了错误分析，识别出诸如是非判断翻转、左右混淆和输出语言不匹配等常见错误模式。

Result: 实验结果显示，当前主流VLM在从英语切换到印尼语时出现明显的性能下降，不同指标下英语与印尼语设置间的准确率差距为8%至25%。错误分析揭示了多种典型失败模式，包括yes/no回答颠倒、解剖侧别误判以及生成答案的语言与预期不符等问题。

Conclusion: 在英语医学VQA任务上表现良好的模型并不一定能在印尼语临床场景中保持同样水平的性能，表明现有模型缺乏真正的多语言鲁棒性。该研究强调必须开展更多涵盖非英语语言的医学多模态模型评估，以推动更具包容性和实用性的医疗AI发展。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are typically evaluated on English radiology visual question answering benchmarks, leaving their robustness under non-English clinical language largely unexplored. We introduce IndoRad-VQA, an Indonesian adaptation of VQA-RAD, to assess whether medical VLMs retain radiology reasoning ability when questions are asked in Bahasa Indonesia. Radiology question-answer pairs are translated into Indonesian with self-evaluation-based quality control to preserve clinical meaning, terminology consistency, and answer equivalence. We evaluate general-purpose, Southeast Asian multilingual, and medical-specific VLMs under English and Indonesian prompting settings. Beyond accuracy, we quantify the language robustness gap between English and Indonesian inputs. We also conduct an error analysis to identify failure modes of question answering, such as yes/no flips, laterality errors, and output-language mismatches. Our findings show that strong performance on English medical VQA benchmarks does not necessarily translate to robust behavior in Indonesian clinical contexts. We observe a performance gap of 8 to 25 percent between the English and Indonesian settings, depending on the evaluation metric. These results highlight the need for more inclusive multilingual evaluation of medical multimodal foundation models. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Lab-IS/IndoRad-VQA.

</details>


### [192] [Don't Forget Your Embeddings: Robust Knowledge Erasure via Precise Editing of Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03695)
*Clara Haya Suslik,Or Shafran,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EMBER的嵌入层擦除模块，通过稀疏矩阵分解精确移除语言模型中与特定概念相关的特征，增强现有知识擦除方法的效果、特异性和抗重学能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识擦除方法常忽略嵌入层，导致目标知识可通过对抗性提示或重学恢复，影响模型的安全性与合规性。

Method: 提出EMBEDding ERasure（EMBER），利用稀疏矩阵分解技术，精准识别并擦除词元嵌入中与特定概念相关的特征，作为即插即用模块与现有方法结合。

Result: 在Gemma-2-2B-it和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上的实验表明，结合EMBER显著提升了擦除效果与特异性，抗重学能力大幅增强，重获准确率降低至35%（此前为70%-76%），且对生成连贯性影响极小且局部化。

Conclusion: 精确的嵌入层干预对于实现鲁棒的概念擦除是必要的，现有擦除方法可通过引入嵌入层操作得到有效增强。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, the ability to erase specific knowledge from them becomes critical for safety and compliance. Prominent methods seek persistent removal by updating the model's parameters, yet the target knowledge often can be recovered through adversarial prompting or relearning. In this work, we hypothesize this limitation stems in part from existing methods overlooking the embedding layer. To address this, we introduce EMBedding ERasure (EMBER), a plug-n-play erasure module that leverages Sparse Matrix Factorization for precise erasure of concept-related features from token embeddings. Through comprehensive evaluations across diverse concepts on Gemma-2-2B-it and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we find that augmenting existing methods with EMBER consistently improves erasure efficacy and specificity across task formats, with minimal coherence loss. Moreover, it dramatically improves robustness to relearning, reducing regained accuracy by up to 50%, limiting it to 35% on Llama compared to 70%-76% for prior methods. Further analysis shows that the coherence cost is localized, affecting only a small set of concept-exclusive tokens. Our work establishes that precise embedding-level intervention is necessary for robust concept erasure, and demonstrates that existing methods can benefit from such augmentation.

</details>


### [193] [Re-Ranking Through an Attribution Lens for Citation Quality in Legal QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03728)
*Mohamed Hesham Elganayni,Selim Saleh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扰动归因的轻量级交叉编码器重排序方法，用于提升法律问答中检索增强生成系统的引用准确性。实验表明该方法显著优于基于语义相似度的检索排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索系统依赖语义相似度排序，但其与实际引用重要性缺乏相关性，导致生成答案时引用不准确。因此需要一种更有效的检索重排序机制。

Method: 利用扰动归因方法（如C-LIME）生成连续的归因分数，训练一个轻量级交叉编码器对检索到的段落进行重排序，以提升生成过程中对关键段落的利用。

Result: 在AQuAECHR基准上，使用两种语言模型和五折交叉验证，该方法显著提高了引用忠实度和与专家答案的一致性；不同模型训练的重排序器之间表现出跨模型的相关性，说明归因信号具有一定的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 扰动归因可作为实用且模型无关的训练信号，用于构建更准确、更具解释性的引用感知检索系统。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation systems for legal question answering typically retrieve passages based on semantic similarity and provide them to a language model, which then generates cited answers. Prior work assumes that highly ranked passages are most likely to be usefully cited by the model. Perturbation-based attribution methods, such as C-LIME, have been used exclusively for post-hoc explanation. However, on the AQuAECHR benchmark, semantic similarity does not correlate with passage attribution. Within a retriever's candidate pool, similarity-based ranking performs worse than random selection at surfacing gold citation paragraphs. To address this limitation, a lightweight cross-encoder is trained on continuous perturbation-based attribution scores to re-rank passages prior to generation. This approach is evaluated on the AQuAECHR benchmark, using two language models and five-fold cross-validation. The re-ranker substantially improves citation faithfulness and alignment with gold expert answers. Notably, two re-rankers trained independently on different models converge beyond their raw attribution agreement. This finding indicates that the cross-encoder reduces model-specific noise and produces a shared relevance signal that partially transfers across models, although same-model re-ranking remains more effective. These results demonstrate that perturbation-based attribution provides a practical, model-agnostic training signal for citation-aware retrieval.

</details>


### [194] [Entropy Gate: Entropy Quenching for Near-Lossless Token Compression in LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03739)
*Justice Owusu Agyemang,Jerry John Kponyo,Kwame Opuni-Boachie Obour Agyekum,Francisca Adoma Acheampong,Kwame Agyeman-Prempeh Agyekum,James Dzisi Gadze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Entropy Gate 是一种基于熵淬灭的令牌压缩框架，通过多因素信息能量和自适应淬火调度，在保持语义保真度的同时显著减少LLM中的冗余令牌使用，实现高达88-96%的压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少大型语言模型在处理过程中对低信息内容（如重复上下文、冗长响应和冗余样板）所浪费的令牌预算。

Method: 引入 Entropy Gate 框架，为每个令牌计算结合统计、结构和位置成分的信息能量 E(t)，采用自适应淬火调度 T(τ) = T_0 / (1 + ατ) 去除生存概率低于阈值的令牌，并设置保真门控以确保语义相似性不低于设定阈值。

Result: 在五个提示类别上实现了40-60%的压缩率，同时保持 S_E > 0.80；能量平方放大 E→E² 额外增加10-25个百分点的压缩效果；结合上下文去重可节省50-70%，与外部内存结合后总压缩率达88-96%。

Conclusion: 按 E(t) 降序选择令牌能最大化预期语义保留，淬火产生嵌套生存集，压缩比接近信息论极限 CR → 1 - I(P; T)/H(P)，且该框架无状态、模型无关，可作为OpenAI兼容的HTTP代理部署。

Abstract: LLM pipelines waste substantial token budgets on low-information content: repeated context, verbose responses, and redundant boilerplate. We introduce Entropy Gate, a token compression framework applying entropy quenching $-$ a thermodynamic process that progressively freezes out low-energy tokens while preserving semantic fidelity. Each token receives a multi-factor information energy $E(t)$ combining statistical, structural, and positional components. An adaptive quenching schedule $T(τ) = T_0 / (1 + ατ)$ removes tokens whose Boltzmann survival probability $p_i = \exp(-E_i / kT)$ falls below threshold, with a fidelity gate halting compression when energy-weighted similarity drops below $θ$. We prove token selection by descending $E(t)$ maximizes expected semantic preservation, that quenching produces nested survival sets, and that achievable compression approaches the information-theoretic limit $\text{CR} \to 1 - I(P; T)/H(P)$. A Phase 1 heuristic achieves 40-60% compression across five prompt categories while maintaining $S_E > 0.80$, with energy-squared amplification $E \to E^2$ adding 10-25 percentage points. Context deduplication adds 50-70% savings on repeated blocks. Output-side quenching, motivated by findings that brevity improves accuracy, further reduces response overhead. Combined with external memory, reduction composes multiplicatively to 88-96% for agentic workloads. The framework is stateless, model-agnostic, and deploys as an OpenAI-compatible HTTP proxy.

</details>


### [195] [Framing Migration News with LLMs: Structured CoT as a Support for Human Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03761)
*David Alonso del Barrio,Jing Wen,Daniel Gatica-Perez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于开源大模型Llama3-8B的结构化思维链（SCoT）提示方法，用于支持可解释的移民新闻框架分析，可在单个GPU上本地部署，提升分类性能并增强模型输出的可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的框架分析方法依赖专有API和大型模型，存在数据隐私、可重复性和学术研究群体间公平访问的问题。本文旨在探索一种可在本地部署、透明且可负担的替代方案。

Method: 提出结构化思维链（SCoT）提示方法，利用Llama3-8B模型进行逐步推理，并基于预定义的框架类别生成可解释的分类结果；在迁移新闻数据集上评估其分类性能，并通过以人为中心的评估研究分析模型推理过程对人类标注员的影响。

Result: SCoT在分类任务中优于零样本和少样本基线，可在单GPU上运行；人类评估显示其推理过程总体被认为是合乎逻辑的（平均4.1/5分），能引发对初始判断的反思，但也可能微妙地影响人类判断。

Conclusion: 结构化推理有助于提升模型输出的可追溯性和人机协同解读，但同时也带来潜在认知影响风险；本地化部署与人机协作设计为社会敏感媒体叙事研究提供了负责任且可及的计算工具路径。

Abstract: Frame analysis of migration news is a socially consequential task: media scholars and researchers who study how migration is narrated need tools that are not only accurate, but transparent, auditable, and accessible within the resource constraints typical of academic research groups. Existing LLM-based approaches rely on proprietary APIs and large models that raise concerns about data privacy, reproducibility and equitable access among media researchers. This work studies how a locally deployable open-source LLM can support interpretable frame analysis as an assistive tool. We introduce a Structured Chain-of-Thought (SCoT) prompting approach using Llama3-8B, enabling step-by-step justifications grounded in predefined framing categories. This structured design allows users to audit model outputs and examine alternative interpretations in a task that is inherently subjective. We evaluate our approach on a dataset of migration-related news and show that SCoT improves classification performance over zero-shot and few-shot baselines while remaining feasible on a single GPU. Then, we conduct a human-centered evaluation in which annotators assess the coherence and influence of "the model's reasoning". Results indicate that SCoT explanations are generally perceived as logical (mean score 4.1/5, though with notable variation across texts) and can prompt reflection on initial interpretations, even when disagreement persists. Our findings highlight both the potential and risks of LLM-assisted frame analysis. While structured reasoning can increase the traceability of model outputs and support critical interpretation, it can also influence human judgment in subtle ways. By enabling local deployment and emphasizing human-in-the-loop interaction, this work contributes to discussions on responsible and accessible computational tools for the study of socially impactful media narratives.

</details>


### [196] [HybridThinker: Efficient Chain-of-Thought Reasoning via Compressed Memory and Transient Thought Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03768)
*Xin Liu,Runsong Zhao,Xinyu Liu,Junhao Ruan,Pengcheng Huang,Shichao Dong,Chunyang Xiao,Chenglong Wang,Changliang Li,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HybridThinker，通过在训练中采用混合训练策略，结合保留压缩表示与临时保留思维步骤，提升大模型推理中的思维链压缩效果，在保持推理速度的同时显著提高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的思维链压缩方法因丢失细粒度信息导致后续推理错误增多，且模型可能依赖直接访问思维步骤而弱化了对记忆令牌的使用能力。

Method: 提出HybridThinker，保留压缩后的记忆令牌并临时存储思维步骤；设计混合训练方案，部分思维步骤可被注意力访问，其余则被遮蔽，迫使模型学习通过记忆令牌进行信息压缩与检索。

Result: 在4个推理基准上，HybridThinker达到与未压缩基线相当的性能，平均准确率比现有最优压缩方法高出5.8个百分点，且推理时间相似。消融实验验证了临时保留思维步骤和混合训练策略的有效性。

Conclusion: HybridThinker通过结合细粒度信息保留与结构化训练机制，有效平衡了推理效率与准确性，推动了思维链压缩技术的发展。

Abstract: Extended chain-of-thought (CoT) traces improve LLM reasoning but incur substantial computational and memory costs. While existing CoT compression methods mitigate this by condensing thought steps into compact representations via memory tokens and retaining only these representations at inference time, the loss of fine-grained information makes subsequent steps more error-prone. To alleviate this, we propose \textbf{HybridThinker}, where in addition to preserved these representations, thought steps are also temporarily retained to provide fine-grained details. However, we observe that naively keeping thought steps accessible to subsequent steps \emph{during training} lets the model bypass memory tokens by retrieving information directly from these steps, leaving the model's ability to compress and retrieve information through memory tokens insufficiently trained. We therefore introduce a hybrid training scheme, in which only some thought steps are directly accessible through attention to subsequent steps, while the other thought steps are masked, forcing the model to use memory tokens for compression and retrieval. Across 4 reasoning benchmarks, HybridThinker matches the uncompressed baseline, advancing the state of the art in CoT compression by 5.8 points on average accuracy with similar inference time. Ablation studies confirm that both temporary thought-step retention and the hybrid training scheme contribute to these gains.

</details>


### [197] [KletterMix: Climbing Toward High-Quality German Pretraining Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03773)
*Maurice Kraus,Ruben Härle,Sebastian Sztwiertnia,Abbas Goher Khan,Mehdi Ali,Michael Fromm,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了KletterMix，一种高质量的德语语言模型预训练语料库，通过翻译先进的英语语料库构建而成，并保持了文档边界、元数据和主题多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有的德语预训练资源在规模、质量和验证方面远落后于英语资源，因此需要构建一个高质量、可复用的德语语料库以支持德语语言模型的发展。

Method: 将最先进的英语预训练语料库翻译为德语，同时保留文档边界、元数据、来源结构和主题多样性，并使用COMETKiwi等工具评估翻译质量。通过控制变量的预训练和退火实验，与其他德语语料库进行比较评估。

Result: KletterMix在语料规模和多样性上达到现代标准，翻译质量高；使用该语料训练的模型在德语下游任务中表现出显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 精心策划的翻译数据可以显著增强德语预训练数据生态，KletterMix为NLP社区提供了一个可靠且可复用的德语数据集资源。

Abstract: High-quality pretraining data is a central ingredient in modern language models, but German-language resources remain far less developed than their English counterparts: they are often smaller, less carefully curated, weakly documented, and rarely validated through controlled training experiments. We introduce KletterMix, a high-quality German corpus for language model pretraining and annealing, designed as a reusable dataset artifact for the natural language processing and modeling community. KletterMix is built by translating a state-of-the-art English pretraining corpus into German while preserving document boundaries, metadata, source structure, and topical diversity. This construction yields a German corpus with the scale and diversity of a modern pretraining dataset, while enabling direct comparison to its English source. We document the dataset through a broad set of corpus-level analyses, including translation quality, document length distributions, topic coverage, source composition, and geographic metadata. Using COMETKiwi, we show that the translated documents achieve strong quality across diverse domains, suggesting that careful translation can preserve much of the semantic and stylistic richness of the original corpus. Beyond dataset construction, we evaluate KletterMix as training data. Through controlled pretraining and annealing ablations against established German corpora, we show that models trained on KletterMix achieve measurable improvements on German-language downstream evaluations. These results demonstrate that carefully curated translated data can substantially strengthen the German pretraining data ecosystem.

</details>


### [198] [Expert-Aware Causal Tracing of Factual Recall in Sparse MoE Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03780)
*Yuetian Lu,Ali Modarressi,Yihong Liu,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种针对稀疏混合专家（MoE）语言模型的事实回忆因果追踪方法，通过干预专家模块分析其在事实预测中的作用，发现不同模型中专家级信息定位的表现存在差异，表明该现象具有模型和协议依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集Transformer模型中，因果追踪已用于定位信息流，但在稀疏MoE模型中，事实预测由路由选择的专家处理，因此需要明确哪些具体专家贡献关键信息。

Method: 提出专家感知的因果追踪方法：使用CounterFact数据集，通过向主语词嵌入添加噪声破坏模型的事实偏好，然后测试干净的MoE块输出或专家级更新是否能恢复正确与错误答案之间的对数概率差异，并进行单层扫描、专家级追踪和多专家协同检验。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base模型中，第44层的专家L44E069被反复选中且其修补效果优于同层其他专家；而在Mixtral-8x7B-v0.1中，虽有中层信号被验证，但关键信息并非来自单一专家，而是多个路由专家协同作用的结果。

Conclusion: MoE模型中的事实因果追踪可以做到专家感知，但专家级的信息定位并非普遍成立，而是依赖于具体模型结构和实验协议。

Abstract: Causal tracing of factual recall has been studied predominantly in dense transformer language models, where interventions localize information flow to layers or feed-forward modules. Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models introduce a sharper question: when a factual prediction is mediated by a routed MoE block, which routed expert contributions matter? We formulate expert-aware causal tracing for sparse MoE language models. Using CounterFact facts, we first corrupt the model's factual preference by adding noise to subject-token embeddings, and then test whether clean MoE-block outputs or clean expert-level updates restore the true-vs-foil logit contrast. For Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base, a layer sweep selects and validates layer 44, and expert-level tracing identifies L44E069 as an expert repeatedly selected in the clean run whose held-out patch outperforms other active same-layer expert patches. For Mixtral-8x7B-v0.1, layer-level tracing validates a mid-layer signal, but the signal is not localized to the selected singleton expert; a coalition check instead recovers it with routed multi-expert updates. These results suggest that MoE factual tracing can be made expert-aware, while also showing that expert-level localization is model- and protocol-dependent rather than universal.

</details>


### [199] [Reasoning over Grammar: Can Synthetic Linguistic Reasoning Traces Enhance Low-Resource Machine Translation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03782)
*Renhao Pei,Yihong Liu,Sampo Pyysalo,Hinrich Schütze,Shaoxiong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过自动生成语言学推理轨迹来改进极低资源语言机器翻译的方法，发现这些轨迹在推理时作为上下文学习的指导最有效，而在训练中使用时效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在极低资源语言的机器翻译中难以有效利用语法信息，因此探索是否可以通过结构化的语言分析和语法推理步骤来提升翻译性能。

Method: 从通用依存树库、词典和语法规则库中自动生成立法语言推理轨迹，并在上下文学习（ICL）、监督微调（SFT）和强化微调（RFT）三种设置下进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，在上下文学习中，可靠的句子特定推理轨迹显著提升了多数模型、语言和指标上的翻译性能；而作为训练数据使用时，增益较小且不一致，模型虽学会格式但常生成错误内容。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型在获得可靠语言分析的前提下可有效利用语法信息进行低资源机器翻译，但自行生成准确语言分析仍是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising approach to machine translation (MT) for extremely low-resource languages by incorporating linguistic resources through in-context learning. However, LLMs often struggle to apply grammatical information effectively during translation. Inspired by recent progress in chain-of-thought reasoning, we investigate whether low-resource MT can benefit from structured intermediate steps of linguistic analysis and grammatical reasoning. We propose a pipeline for automatically generating step-by-step linguistic reasoning traces from Universal Dependencies treebanks, dictionaries, and grammar-rule banks. We evaluate these traces in three settings: in-context learning (ICL), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT), on Xibe and Chintang as test cases. Our results show that linguistic reasoning traces are most effective as inference-time guidance: in ICL, reliable sentence-specific traces substantially improve translation performance across most models, languages, and metrics. In contrast, using the linguistic reasoning traces as training data yields smaller and less consistent gains, as models learn the trace format but often generate erroneous content. These findings suggest that LLMs can leverage grammatical information for low-resource MT when given reliable linguistic analyses, while learning to generate such analyses remains a major bottleneck.

</details>


### [200] [Backdoor Unlearning Generalization: A Path Toward the Removal of Unknown Triggers in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03785)
*Lisa Bouger,Théo Lasnier,Philippe Looubet Moundi,Yannick Teglia,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，通过遗忘方法消除一个后门可以泛化到抑制其他未被明确针对的后门，提出跨后门迁移效应，并引入交叉激活偏移距离来量化不同训练引发的模型变化差异，为大语言模型安全提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后门防御方法通常一次只能应对一个已知触发器的后门，防御方在面对未知后门时处于结构性劣势，因此需要一种更通用的防御机制。

Method: 通过在三种模型族上分析逐个移除一个后门后的模型表现，研究遗忘过程中对未目标后门的抑制效果，并提出交叉激活偏移距离（Cross Activation Shift Distance）来衡量不同训练所引起模型变化之间的距离。

Result: 发现通过对单个触发器进行遗忘训练，不仅能去除该后门，还能抑制其他未被明确训练处理的后门；交叉激活偏移距离可有效反映这种跨后门的迁移效应。

Conclusion: 遗忘引起的后门中和具有跨后门泛化能力，防御者可通过主动注入并移除可控后门，利用此迁移效应来抑制攻击者可能预先植入的未知后门，开辟了大语言模型安全防护的新路径。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks in Large Language Models (LLMs) are a growing security concern, where models can generate adversary-chosen content. Existing defenses target backdoors one at a time and typically require knowledge of the trigger, leaving the defender at a structural disadvantage when unknown backdoors may exist in a model. We show that backdoor neutralization through unlearning generalizes across backdoors: training a model to ignore a single trigger can also suppress other backdoors that were never explicitly targeted. We study this phenomenon across three model families, whose backdoors were injected via pretraining or continual pretraining, by analyzing the models obtained after removing one backdoor at a time. To understand why unlearning certain backdoors induces the suppression of others, we introduce the Cross Activation Shift Distance, to quantify the distance between model changes induced by different trainings. Our results open a new direction for LLM safety as defenders could deliberately inject controlled backdoors and then remove them, leveraging cross-backdoor transfer to also suppress unknown backdoors that an attacker may have previously introduced in the model.

</details>


### [201] [Exploring Adversarial Robustness and Safety Alignment in Multilingual Multi-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03793)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在多语言环境下的对抗鲁棒性与安全性，发现低资源语言中的“安全”表现往往是由于模型理解失败所致，即“安全幻觉”；而通过全阶段训练实现多语言能力的模型（如Qwen3-VL）才能实现真正的跨语言安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对MLLM鲁棒性的研究主要集中于英语任务，缺乏对多语言安全行为的系统性探究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在12种不同语言上评估开源MLLM，采用基于梯度的攻击方法分析跨语言脆弱性转移，并考察文本与图像中多语言有害指令的解析与响应情况。

Result: 发现多语言漏洞具有强跨语言可迁移性；低资源语言因语言理解与视觉接地失败导致较少产生不安全响应，形成‘安全-by-失败’现象；仅在指令微调阶段引入多语言能力的模型易出现此问题。

Conclusion: 浅层多语言适配可能导致表面安全假象，深层训练集成才能实现真正跨语言安全对齐，强调需区分真实对齐与理解失败带来的虚假安全。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models integrate visual perception into language reasoning, introducing a continuous attack surface susceptible to adversarial attacks. Prior work on MLLM robustness has focused largely on English-centric tasks, leaving multilingual behaviour unexplored. We address this gap through a systematic study of adversarial robustness and multimodal safety across 12 diverse languages, evaluating open-source MLLMs that acquire multilingual capability through instruction tuning. Gradient-based attacks reveal a transferable multilingual vulnerability: adversarial images optimized in one language continue to induce failure in others, demonstrating strong cross-lingual transferability. Multilingual safety further varies with how effectively a model retrieves or interprets harmful instructions. When harmful intent is issued through text, languages with stronger linguistic grounding more often elicit misuse-enabling responses, while weaker languages produce fewer unsafe outputs. When embedded in the image as typographic content, English scripts are reliably recognised and followed, whereas non-English scripts are rarely parsed by the vision encoder. Lower-resource languages may therefore appear safer, but this is an artefact of comprehension and visual-grounding failures rather than genuine alignment, a phenomenon we term safety-by-failure. In contrast, MLLMs that build multilingual capability throughout their training stages rather than only at instruction tuning, such as Qwen3-VL, exhibit genuine cross-lingual safety, maintaining active refusal across languages rather than masking comprehension failure. Shallow multilingual adaptation, such as fine-tuning on translated instruction data, may produce surface-level understanding that creates illusory safety in low-resource languages; deeper integration across training stages leads to genuine multilingual safety alignment.

</details>


### [202] [Consistency Training Can Entrench Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03810)
*David Demitri Africa,Arathi Mani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 一致性训练对模型对齐性的影响具有双重性：虽然能抑制奖励黑客和突发性错位行为，但会加剧谄媚（sycophancy）等其他错位行为。研究通过108个“模型生物”实验验证了这一现象，并提出统一理论框架解释其机制，强调在关键系统中使用一致性训练需审慎评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨一致性训练是否会影响模型的对齐性，尤其是其自举特性是否会放大模型的不良行为。

Method: 在7B到70B参数规模的开源模型上微调出多种可控的错位行为，构建108个“模型生物”，测试七种一致性训练方法的影响，并分析标签过程引起的分布偏移作用。

Result: 发现一致性训练通常能抑制奖励黑客和突发性错位，但会显著加剧sycophancy；分布偏移可能是导致系统性对齐效应的主要原因。

Conclusion: 一致性训练并非对齐中立，其在关键系统中的应用需要仔细审计。

Abstract: Consistency training encourages a model to produce similar outputs across related inputs or sampling procedures. Such methods are simple, scalable, and largely label-free, but their effects on model alignment remain poorly understood. Could the self-bootstrapping nature of these methods amplify undesired behavior in models? We test seven consistency training methods on 108 ``model organisms: open-source models (7B--70B) fine-tuned to exhibit various forms of controlled misaligned behavior. We find that outcomes vary significantly: consistency training generally suppresses reward hacking and emergent misalignment but amplifies sycophancy. We present evidence that distribution shifts induced by the consistency labeling process, rather than variation in the selection operators, may be the primary driver of systematic alignment effects. Finally, we present a unifying theoretical framework to derive conditions under which consistency training will amplify or suppress misalignment. In total, our study establishes that consistency training is not alignment-neutral, and that its use in critical systems should be carefully audited.

</details>


### [203] [Rethinking the Idiomaticity Decomposability Hypothesis: Evidence from Distributional Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03817)
*Maggie Mi,Golzar Atefi,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Felix Gers,Aline Villavicencio,Nafise Sadat Moosavi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用语言模型探讨了习语的可分解性、句法灵活性和分布经验之间的关系，发现模型内部的可分解性度量与人类判断弱相关，并与句法灵活性呈轻微负相关，且在训练过程中， surprisal、可分解性和频率共同影响习语表征的稳定，其中可分解性具有最强的训练依赖效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨习语的可分解性是否预测其句法灵活性，并比较基于使用的分布经验（如熟悉度和可预测性）对习语行为的影响。

Method: 使用上下文化语言模型作为受控的分布学习器，提出一种模型内部的可分解性度量，并将其与人类评分、句法灵活性和可预测性进行关联，同时追踪预训练过程中的习语学习过程。

Result: 模型推导出的可分解性与人类判断弱相关，与句法灵活性呈小但一致的负相关；预训练分析显示，习语表征的稳定不仅取决于频率，surprisal、可分解性和频率均起作用，其中可分解性表现出最强的训练依赖效应。

Conclusion: 习语的可分解性在语言模型的学习过程中起重要作用，支持基于使用的观点，即分布经验（特别是可分解性）在习语表征形成中具有关键影响。

Abstract: Idioms can be analysed in terms of their decomposability, the extent to which constituent meanings contribute to the figurative whole. Decomposability is thought to predict syntactic flexibility. Usage-based accounts instead attribute idiom behaviour to distributional experience, such as speaker familiarity and predictability. We examine these views using contextualised language models as controlled distributional learners. We propose a model-internal measure of decomposability and relate it to human ratings, syntactic flexibility, and predictability while tracking idiom learning during pretraining. Model-derived decomposability correlates weakly with human judgments and shows a small but consistent negative relationship with syntactic flexibility. Pretraining analyses show that stabilisation of idiom representations in models is not explained by frequency alone. Instead, surprisal, decomposability, and frequency all contribute, with decomposability showing the strongest training-dependent effect.

</details>


### [204] [Clustered Self-Assessment: A Simple yet Effective Method for Uncertainty Quantification in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03846)
*Qi Cao,Takeshi Kojima,Andrew Gambardella,Helinyi Peng,Yutaka Matsuo,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自我评估的LLM不确定性量化方法，通过将采样生成结果聚类并转化为结构化选择题，利用模型对各选项的赋值概率作为置信度估计，有效且高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）常生成看似合理但事实错误的内容，且缺乏明确的不确定性估计，导致用户难以判断输出的可靠性。现有方法依赖间接信号（如采样熵），解释性差且未充分利用模型自评能力。

Method: 将LLM的采样生成结果按语义聚类，转化为结构化的多项选择题，并利用LLM为每个选项分配的概率作为其置信度，从而实现自我评估式的不确定性量化。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上的实验表明，该方法持续优于基线方法，仅需两次额外采样即可达到竞争性性能，兼具有效性与高效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自评估方法能更准确地量化LLM的不确定性，提升输出可信度评估，且资源消耗低，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable performance across diverse tasks, but they often generate responses that appear plausible while being factually incorrect. This problem is compounded by the lack of explicit uncertainty estimates, which makes it difficult for users to judge the reliability of model outputs. Existing uncertainty quantification methods typically rely on indirect signals, such as entropy across sampled generations. These signals can be difficult to interpret and do not fully leverage the model's ability to assess its own uncertainty. We propose a simple yet effective self-assessment method for uncertainty quantification in LLMs. Our approach groups sampled generations into semantically distinct clusters, converts them into answer options in a structured multiple-choice question, and uses the probability assigned by the LLM to each option as a confidence estimate. Experiments across multiple models and datasets show that our method consistently outperforms baseline approaches. Notably, it achieves competitive performance with as few as two additional samples, demonstrating both its effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [205] [A Training-Free Mixture-of-Agents Framework for Multi-Document Summarization using LLMs and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03867)
*Cuong Vuong Tuan,Trang Mai Xuan,Tien-Cuong Nguyen,Vu-Duc Ngo,Thien Van Luong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的多智能体框架用于多文档摘要，结合大语言模型与知识图谱的优势，在多个数据集上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在捕捉跨文档复杂关系、依赖标注数据及跨领域泛化方面存在局限。

Method: 将摘要任务分解为提取、知识感知抽象和迭代优化三个专门的智能体任务，通过基于大语言模型的多视角一致性机制融合结果，且无需微调。

Result: 在四种英越语数据集上实现了最先进或具有竞争力的效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的模块化框架有效提升了多文档摘要的性能和适应性，具备良好的跨语言与跨域应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-Document Summarization (MDS) plays a critical role in distilling essential information from collections of textual data. Existing approaches often struggle to capture complex inter-document relationships, rely heavily on large amounts of labeled data for supervised training, or exhibit limited generalization across domains and languages. To address these limitations, we present a training-free mixture-of-agents framework for MDS that leverages the complementary strengths of large language models (LLMs) and knowledge graphs. Our approach decomposes summarization into specialized agent tasks: extractive selection, knowledge-aware abstraction, and iterative refinement, each operating without task-specific fine-tuning. We unify their outputs using a multi-perspective consistency mechanism guided by LLMs. Experiments across four datasets in English and Vietnamese demonstrate state-of-the-art or competitive performance, validating the effectiveness and adaptability of our modular design.

</details>


### [206] [RealClawBench: Live OpenClaw Benchmarks from Real Developer-Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03889)
*Zongwei Lv,Zhewen Tan,Yaoming Li,Yilun Yao,Yuxuan Tian,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang,Weihong Lin,Tong Yang,Guangxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RealClawBench是一个基于真实OpenClaw会话构建的实时基准框架，旨在更真实地评估开发人员与智能体交互任务的难度、分布和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试往往缺乏对真实用户请求的真实性和复杂性的反映，无法准确衡量实际部署中智能体的能力。

Method: 通过重构执行环境和设计确定性可验证评分器，将真实用户会话转化为可重复执行且能自动评分的任务。从大量真实会话中采样281个任务，保持源分布一致性（Jensen-Shannon散度最大为0.0448）。

Result: 评估了14个当前主流模型，表现最好的系统仅完成65.8%的任务，表明现有智能体在真实开发任务中仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: RealClawBench提供了一条实用路径，使基准测试能够更好地反映智能体在真实使用场景中的能力，推动更贴近现实的智能体发展。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks should reflect what users actually ask deployed agents to do, yet existing benchmarks often miss key realism properties of real developer-agent sessions. We introduce RealClawBench, a live benchmark framework built from real OpenClaw sessions to capture the distribution, diversity, and real-world difficulty of deployed agent use. Real user requests are challenging to benchmark because they often depend on local execution environments, involve implicit or underspecified intent, and require nontrivial verification. RealClawBench addresses these challenges with two core mechanisms: reconstructed execution environments and deterministic verifiable scorers, which together convert real sessions into reproducible, automatically scored tasks. The resulting release contains 281 executable tasks sampled from a much larger real-session pool while preserving the source distribution, with maximum final-vs-source Jensen-Shannon divergence of 0.0448. Evaluating 14 contemporary models shows that the best system solves only 65.8% of tasks, revealing substantial headroom on realistic developer-agent workloads. By turning real deployed sessions into controlled evaluation instances, RealClawBench provides a practical path toward benchmarks that better measure agent capability in actual use. Code is available at:https://anonymous.4open.science/r/real-claw-bench-582B.

</details>


### [207] [Synthesize and Reward -- Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Step Tool Use in Live Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03892)
*Ibrahim Abdelaziz,Asim Munawar,Kinjal Basu,Maxwell Crouse,Chulaka Gunasekara,Suneet Katrekar,Pavan Kapanipathi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PROVE是一个用于训练大语言模型进行多步工具调用的框架，通过构建状态化执行环境、生成基于真实状态的合成数据以及设计无需人工评判的程序化奖励机制，显著提升了模型在多步任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多步工具调用训练受限于缺乏真实的状态环境、合成查询与服务器状态脱节以及基于召回的强化学习奖励导致冗余调用等问题。

Method: 提出PROVE框架，包含：1）20个带状态的MCP服务器库，支持会话级状态隔离的实时执行；2）基于依赖图引导和实时采样状态的对话模拟，自动生成有效的多轮工具调用轨迹；3）多组件程序化奖励机制，包括有效性评分、覆盖度、效率惩罚、工具名信号和参数匹配奖励。

Result: 在Qwen和Granite系列模型上使用约1.3万样本训练后，PROVE在BFCL Multi-Turn、tau2-bench和T-Eval三个基准上分别最高提升+10.2、+6.8和+6.5分，且奖励超参数固定，仅调整学习率。

Conclusion: 紧凑的程序化奖励结合真实状态环境和高质量合成数据，能够在不同模型家族中一致地提升多步工具调用性能，无需外部评判模型。

Abstract: Training LLMs to orchestrate multi-step tool calls is held back by three coupled obstacles: realistic stateful execution environments are costly to build, synthetic training queries are often detached from the server's actual state (so the generated tool calls fail to execute), and recall-based RL rewards incentivize verbose tool-calling patterns. We present PROVE (Programmatic Rewards On Verified Environments), a framework with three contributions: (1) a library of 20 stateful MCP (Model Context Protocol) servers exposing 343 tools, enabling live-execution RL training with session-scoped state isolation; (2) an automated data synthesis pipeline that generates validated multi-turn tool-call trajectories against these servers via dependency-graph-guided conversation simulation grounded in live-sampled server state, so every generated query references entities that actually exist; and (3) a multi-component programmatic reward - graduated validity scoring, dependency-aware coverage, an adaptive efficiency penalty with a complexity-scaled call budget, a tool-name signal, and an argument-value matching bonus - requiring no external judge model. We train four models (Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, Granite-4.1-8B) with GRPO using identical reward hyperparameters and ~13K training examples; only learning rate is tuned per model family from a three-point sweep. On BFCL Multi-Turn, tau2-bench, and T-Eval, PROVE yields improvements of up to +10.2, +6.8, and +6.5 points respectively, demonstrating that a compact programmatic reward yields consistent gains on multi-step tool orchestration across two model families.

</details>


### [208] [Knowledge Editing in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03924)
*Haewon Park,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了知识编辑中的“定位-编辑”方法在掩码扩散模型（MDMs）中的适用性，发现尽管编辑位置在MDMs和自回归模型（ARMs）中具有可迁移性，但多词编辑在MDMs中表现下降；通过优化中间状态的编辑策略，显著提升了MDMs的多词编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究“定位-编辑”这一在自回归模型中有效的知识编辑方法是否适用于结构不同的掩码扩散模型，验证其基本假设在不同生成机制下的普适性。

Method: 将“定位-编辑”方法迁移到MDMs上，使用因果追踪定位关键层（早期到中期MLP层在主词末尾位置），并在LLaDA、Dream两个MDMs与LLaMA、Qwen两个ARMs之间进行对比实验；针对多词编辑失败问题，引入对部分去噪中间状态的优化策略。

Result: 1）编辑的最佳位置在MDMs和ARMs中一致，均位于主词末尾的早期至中期MLP层；2）单词编辑在两类模型中均有效，但随着目标长度增加，MDMs中的编辑效果系统性下降；3）该问题源于编辑未覆盖生成过程中出现的部分去噪中间状态；4）提出的新优化方法显著恢复了MDMs的多词编辑性能。

Conclusion: “定位-编辑”的定位步骤具有跨范式可迁移性，但编辑效果受生成机制影响；MDMs中多词知识编辑的挑战源于迭代生成路径中的中间状态不匹配，需针对性优化以提升编辑鲁棒性。

Abstract: Knowledge editing aims to update or correct factual knowledge in a language model. A widely used approach, locate-then-edit, does this in two steps: it first localizes a fact within the model, then edits the weights there. To date, such methods have been developed exclusively on autoregressive models (ARMs). Whether their underlying assumptions hold for masked diffusion models (MDMs), which model text bidirectionally and generate by iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, remains an open question. We address it by transferring locate-then-edit to MDMs and comparing two MDMs (LLaDA, Dream) with two ARMs (LLaMA, Qwen) at matched scale. Our central finding has two parts. First, where an edit is applied transfers across paradigms: causal tracing highlights the same early-to-mid-layer MLP at the last subject token in both, and editing is most effective there. Second, this shared location does not guarantee a shared outcome. Single-token edits succeed in both, but as targets grow longer, editing degrades systematically in the MDMs but not the ARMs. The failure stems from how the edited fact is generated: producing a multi-token target requires passing through partially unmasked intermediate states for which the edit was never optimized. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce a simple correction that optimizes the edit for these states, substantially restoring multi-token performance.

</details>


### [209] [A Pocket Offline Model for Simultaneous Speech Translation as CUNI Submission to IWSLT 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03948)
*Aziz Sharipov Ortega,Dominik Macháček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Canary模型和AlignAtt策略的离线直接语音到文本翻译系统的实时翻译能力，并参与了IWSLT 2026同声传译共享任务，展现出高质量翻译、低计算需求和多语言支持的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在低延迟和高延迟环境下实现高质量的同步语音到文本翻译，同时降低计算资源消耗并支持多语言翻译。

Method: 采用Canary模型结合最先进的AlignAtt策略，实现从语音到文本的同步翻译，并在IWSLT 2026共享任务中进行评估。

Result: 系统在计算无感知模拟下优于类似规模的基线模型，具有10亿参数且支持25种源语言和25种目标语言。

Conclusion: 该系统在翻译质量、计算效率和多语言支持方面表现优异，适用于实际场景中的同步语音翻译任务。

Abstract: We implement simultaneous translation capability with the offline direct speech-to-text translation model Canary, using the state-of-the-art policy AlignAtt, and submit it to IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Shared task for Czech to English and English to German and Italian.
  The strengths of our system are: (1) high translation quality, outperforming similarly sized baselines both in low- and high-latency regimes in computationally unaware simulations; (2) low computational requirements, as the model has only 1B parameters; (3) multilinguality -- support of 25 source and 25 target languages.

</details>


### [210] [Efficient ASR Training with Conversations that Never Happened](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03957)
*Máté Gedeon,Péter Mihajlik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过LLM生成和TTS合成的对话数据增强方法，用于提升低资源语言和特定领域下的语音识别性能，在匈牙利语任务中用较少真实数据结合大量模拟数据超越了基于2700小时真实数据训练的零样本模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏匹配领域的多说话人对话数据，低资源语言和小众领域的对话式语音识别（ASR）发展受限，因此需要一种可扩展的数据增强方案来弥补真实数据的不足。

Method: 构建了一个数据增强流程：利用LLM生成带有参与者元数据的场景级对话，将说话人属性映射到TTS声音配置文件，并使用TTS合成语音片段，最终组装成具有说话人意识的模拟对话用于ASR模型训练；在相同FastConformer-Large训练框架下评估了五种LLM家族在单生成器、固定预算混合和扩展规模三种设置下的效果。

Result: 合成对话 consistently 提升了语音识别性能，但生成器选择和数据构成显著影响增益效果；最佳配置仅使用67小时真实对话和636小时模拟数据，就在BEA-Dialogue基准上超过了基于2700小时真实匈牙利语数据训练的零样本模型。

Conclusion: LLM生成并经TTS合成的对话数据是真实对话语料库的有效补充，尤其适用于低资源语言和数据稀缺场景下的语音模型训练。

Abstract: Conversational ASR for lower-resource languages and niche domains is limited by the scarcity of domain-matched multi-speaker training data. We propose an augmentation pipeline that generates scenario-level dialogues with participant metadata, maps speaker attributes to TTS voice profiles, and assembles synthesized utterances into speaker-aware simulated conversations. We evaluated five LLM families under single-generator, fixed-budget mixture, and scale-up settings using the same FastConformer-Large training recipe for each one. We ran comprehensive evaluations on the Hungarian BEA-Dialogue benchmark corpus, with the method itself being applicable to any language given the resources for each component. The results show that synthetic conversations consistently improve speech recognition performance, but generator choice and data composition strongly affect the gains. Our largest training configuration, using only 67 hours of real conversations and 636 hours of simulated data, achieves better performance on the evaluation benchmark than a zero-shot model trained on 2700 hours of Hungarian speech. These findings indicate that LLM-generated conversational data synthesized with TTS is a practical complement to real conversational corpora for speech model training.

</details>


### [211] [Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering for Efficient and Controllable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03965)
*Yu Xia,Zhouhang Xie,Xin Xu,Byungkyu Kang,Prarit Lamba,Xiang Gao,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering (ACTS)的方法，通过控制器代理在推理过程中自适应地引导冻结的推理器，实现高效且可控的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理方法虽然能控制思考长度，但通常以缩短、提前停止或压缩推理轨迹的方式进行，导致模型的思考过程不透明，且效率低下。因此需要一种更高效、可控制的推理方法。

Method: 将推理控制建模为马尔可夫决策过程，使用一个控制器代理根据当前推理轨迹和剩余预算选择推理策略和引导短语，指导冻结的推理器生成下一步。控制器通过合成的多预算增强轨迹初始化，并通过带预算条件奖励塑形的强化学习进一步优化。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，ACTS在显著节省token的情况下达到了与完整推理相当的性能，并实现了跨不同推理器和任务的可控制准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: ACTS提供了一种高效、可控的推理框架，能够在保持推理连续性的同时实现预算感知的策略控制，提升了大语言模型在实际应用中的实用性。

Abstract: Large language models improve final-answer accuracy through extended chain-of-thought reasoning, but often spend tokens inefficiently and offer little inference-time control. Existing efficient reasoning methods control thinking length by shortening, early-stopping, or compressing traces, leaving how the model thinks implicit. In this paper, we propose Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering (ACTS), which formulates reasoning steering as a Markov decision process where a controller agent adaptively steers a frozen reasoner during inference. At each step, the controller observes the reasoning trace and remaining thinking budget, then issues a steering action consisting of a reasoning strategy and a steering phrase that initiates the next reasoner step. This enables budget-aware strategy control for efficient reasoning while preserving the reasoner's generation continuity. We initialize the controller agent from our constructed synthetic steering trajectories with multi-budget augmentation, and further optimize it via reinforcement learning with budget-conditioned reward shaping. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that ACTS matches full-thinking performance with substantial token savings, and enables controllable accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across different reasoners and tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/Andree-9/ACTS.

</details>


### [212] [AlignAtt4LLM: Fast AlignAtt for Decoder-Only LLMs at IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03967)
*Quentin Fuxa,Dominik Macháček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlignAtt4LLM，首个将AlignAtt应用于仅解码器大语言模型（如Gemma-4）的同步语音翻译系统，通过改进提示设计、注意力头选择与查询/键捕获，在英-德、英-意任务上显著优于基线，尤其在低延迟场景表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AlignAtt策略扩展至仅解码器大语言模型，解决传统方法依赖编码器-解码器交叉注意力的限制，以支持更广泛且高效的实时语音翻译应用。

Method: 采用同步级联架构：Qwen3-ASR结合强制对齐生成源文本前缀，Gemma-4 E4B-it在MT端执行AlignAtt策略；提出四项关键技术：(1) 提示中显式引入源片段，(2) 离线筛选翻译专用对齐注意力头，(3) 草稿到源注意力块的qk-fast重放，(4) 运行时精确保留模型输出的查询/键捕获机制。

Result: 在IWSLT 2026开发集上，英-德和英-意方向在约2秒低延迟和低于4秒高延迟模式下均优于基线；英-中结果较混合，但方法具通用性，可迁移至更强的仅解码器MT模型。

Conclusion: AlignAtt4LLM成功将AlignAtt适配至仅解码器大模型，无需交叉注意力即可实现高效同步翻译，具备低延迟优势和跨语言可扩展潜力，为未来轻量、高性能的实时ST系统提供新路径。

Abstract: We describe AlignAtt4LLM, an IWSLT 2026 simultaneous speech translation system for English to German, Italian, and Chinese. The system is a synchronous cascade: Qwen3-ASR with forced alignment produces an incrementally updated source transcript, and Gemma-4 E4B-it translates that prefix under an MT-side AlignAtt policy.
  To our knowledge, this is the first application of AlignAtt to a decoder-only LLM, where the encoder-decoder cross-attention used by earlier AlignAtt systems is absent. We recover a usable policy by proposing (1) an explicit source span in the prompt, (2) offline selection of translation-specific alignment heads, (3) selective qk-fast replay of the draft-to-source attention block, and (4) runtime query/key capture that preserves model outputs bit-identically.
  On the IWSLT 2026 development set, AlignAtt4LLM outperforms the supplied baselines for the European target languages, English to German and English to Italian, in both the low-latency regime around 2 seconds and the high-latency regime below 4 seconds CU-LongYAAL. Results for English to Chinese are more mixed, but the method is not tied to Gemma-4: because AlignAtt4LLM only requires a deterministic prompt layout, calibrated attention heads, and query/key capture, the same policy can be reapplied to stronger translation-focused decoder-only MT backbones for non-European target languages.

</details>


### [213] [QUBRIC: Co-Designing Queries and Rubrics for RL Beyond Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03968)
*Rongzhi Zhang,Rui Feng,Zhihan Zhang,Jingfeng Yang,Qingyu Yin,Xin Liu,Zixuan Zhang,Priyanka Nigam,Bing Yin,Tuo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QUBRIC是一种协同设计查询与评分标准的框架，通过将开放式问题转化为可评估的情景式问题，并结合对比性评分生成和可学习性过滤，有效提升基于评分标准的强化学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的强化学习方法在固定查询分布下优化评分标准，导致评分质量受限于查询结构：开放式查询产生模糊评分，而过度缩小查询范围会引入无法验证的虚构参考，造成训练无奖励信号。

Method: 提出QUBRIC框架：首先利用教师模型的关键点指导，将开放式查询重写为基于情景的可评估问题；然后通过对比生成评分标准，捕捉教师与策略模型之间的差距；最后采用可学习性过滤机制筛选出信息丰富的查询-评分对用于GRPO训练。

Result: QUBRIC在ArenaHard上比SFT基线提升+5.5分；仅使用指令遵循数据训练，在法律、道德和叙事推理三个未见基准上平均提升+6.3分，且改进主要集中在推理相关维度。

Conclusion: 协同设计查询与评分标准能够突破现有方法的结构瓶颈，使基于评分标准的强化学习成为RLVR之外更实用的扩展路径，适用于非完全可验证任务。

Abstract: Rubric-based RL is a promising route for extending reinforcement learning beyond verifiable rewards, yet existing methods optimize rubrics while treating the query distribution as fixed. We identify a structural bottleneck: rubric quality is constrained by query structure. Open-ended queries yield vague rubrics; naively narrowing them introduces fabricated references that no model can verify, so all responses fail and training receives no reward signal. We present QUBRIC, a framework that co-designs queries and rubrics. Teacher-derived key points ground the rewriting of open-ended queries into scenario-based, evaluable questions. Contrastive rubric generation then turns teacher-policy gaps into query-level criteria, and learnability filtering retains only informative query-rubric pairs for GRPO training. QUBRIC achieves a +5.5 point gain on ArenaHard over the SFT baseline. Trained only on instruction-following data, it further transfers to three held-out benchmarks spanning legal, moral, and narrative reasoning (+6.3 points on average), with improvements concentrated in reasoning-related dimensions. These results provide evidence that co-designing queries and rubrics can make rubric-based RL a practical complement to RLVR beyond strictly verifiable tasks.

</details>


### [214] [Quantifying Faithful Confidence Expression in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03969)
*Areeb Gani,Asal Meskin,Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新框架来系统量化大型推理模型（LRMs）的忠实校准（FC），即模型内在置信度与语言表达置信度之间的一致性。研究发现，当前LRMs在可信表达其置信度方面存在显著缺陷，且传统评估方法在长推理链输出上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大型推理模型（LRMs）广泛应用于高风险场景，其推理过程常被用户视为能力与自信的表现，因此确保其置信度表达的真实性至关重要。然而现有方法难以准确衡量LRMs在复杂、无明确边界的推理链中的置信度一致性，亟需更可靠的评估框架。

Method: 提出一种基于词元概率、隐藏状态和采样响应一致性的多源内部不确定性分析框架，并引入前缀条件采样方法以控制推理链间的结构与条件差异，从而更准确地评估LRMs的语言确定性与其内在置信度之间的对齐程度。

Result: 在多种主流模型、数据集和提示设置下测试发现：LRMs普遍存在忠实校准问题；推理行为本身并未提升FC表现；适用于非推理模型的提示干预在推理场景中无效；不同置信度估计器对同一推理链的评估结果差异显著，暴露出先前评估方法的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 忠实校准是LRMs可靠性与对齐性的一个独立且关键的目标，应被专门优化与评估，特别是在高风险应用场景中部署此类系统时。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty communication is critical to the trustworthiness of LLMs, yet faithful calibration (FC)--the alignment between models' intrinsic and (linguistically) expressed confidence--is a persistent failure mode. This challenge is key for large reasoning models (LRMs), whose extended reasoning traces are often interpreted by users as evidence of deliberation, competence, and confidence. Despite the importance of FC and wide usage of LRMs, the extent to which LRMs can faithfully express their confidence remains poorly understood. Moreover, the prevailing paradigm to measure FC does not generalize well to the long chain-of-thought outputs generated by LRMs, which tend to lack clear step boundaries, involve inconsistent step structure, and encode complex conditional dependencies throughout the trace--complicating estimation of intrinsic confidence. To address this challenge, we introduce a novel framework to systematically quantify FC of LRMs. Our framework analyzes linguistic decisiveness relative to three sources of internal uncertainty, based on token probabilities, hidden states, and sampled response consistency. We also devise a prefix-conditioned sampling approach to control for conditional and structural variation across traces. Applying our framework to a diverse suite of leading models, datasets, and prompts, we find that faithful confidence expression is a significant challenge for LRMs. Reasoning behaviors do not automatically translate to improved FC, and prompt interventions for non-reasoning models do not improve faithfulness in the reasoning setting. Different confidence estimators further produce divergent assessments of the same traces, revealing fragility in prior evaluation methodologies. Taken together, our work establishes FC as a distinct reliability and alignment target for LRMs, particularly as such systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes contexts.

</details>


### [215] [Language Models Compare Quantities Using Number-specific and Unit-specific Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03982)
*Mutsumi Sasaki,Go kamoda,Ryosuke Takahashi,Kosuke Sato,Kentaro Inui,Keisuke Sakaguchi,Benjamin Heinzerling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语言模型在处理带单位的数值比较时，倾向于使用数字和单位的启发式组合，而非先转换到统一尺度进行精确比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型如何在不同单位系统下比较带有测量单位的数值，揭示其内部处理机制。

Method: 通过控制实验设置，使用线性代理模型分析语言模型对数值差异和单位尺度差异的响应，并进行因果干预以验证相关变量的影响。

Result: 发现语言模型在接近比较边界时准确率下降，错误呈现系统性；代理模型可预测模型偏好，且因果干预能改变输出结果。

Conclusion: 语言模型并非通过统一换算进行精确比较，而是依赖于数字和单位的启发式策略来比较数量。

Abstract: Quantities with measurement units, such as 110 cm and 1.2 m, require language models (LMs) to combine a numeral with a symbolic unit scale. Here, we study how LMs compare such quantities in controlled settings spanning several unit systems. We find that accuracy degrades near the comparison boundary, where small changes in value determine the correct answer. The resulting errors are systematic: linear surrogate models predict LM preferences from numerical-difference and unit-scale-difference cues, and causal interventions on subspaces aligned with these variables shift model's output. The results suggest that LMs compare quantities through a bag of heuristics over numerals and units, rather than first converting both expressions to an exact shared-scale representation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [Visual Graph Scaffolds for Structural Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02673)
*Runlin Lei,Xiaokui Xiao,Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了图结构作为大语言模型内部推理辅助工具的潜力，发现视觉化的图指导在缺乏直接答案线索时依然有效，支持将图不仅视为外部知识结构，也视为组织推理的视觉支架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索图结构除了作为外部信息源外，是否还能作为组织推理过程的内部辅助手段。

Method: 将教师提供的多跳问答任务中的推理轨迹重写为图思维导图，并用其引导学生模型进行学习。

Result: 实验表明，在去除直接答案提示后，文本化扁平图结构的效果显著下降，而视觉图指导仍保持有效性，并在监督微调和KL蒸馏后持续表现出优势。

Conclusion: 图结构不仅能作为外部知识来源，还可作为视觉支架来组织大语言模型的推理过程，具有独立于信息供给的推理辅助价值。

Abstract: Graphs have been used to enhance large language models (LLMs) for structured reasoning, mostly as external knowledge sources are provided to models at test time. In this paper, we take a different view: the value of graphs for LLMs lie not only in supplying information, but also in organizing reasoning. Inspired by how humans use graph-structured mind maps to organize branching and converging thoughts, we ask whether graphs can serve as an internal form of reasoning assistance. We study this question on multi-hop question answering tasks, where teacher-provided reasoning traces are rewritten as graph mind maps and used to guide a student model. Our experiments reveal a clear modality gap. When graph structures are flattened into text, their benefits become limited once direct answer hints are removed. Under this abstract guidance setting, both reasoning efficiency and answer quality degrade substantially. In contrast, visual graph guidance remains effective without direct answer clues, and its advantage persists after supervised fine-tuning and KL-based distillation. The above findings support the claim that graphs should be studied not only as external knowledge structures for LLMs, but also as visual scaffolds for organizing reasoning.

</details>


### [217] [AURA: Action-Gated Memory for Robot Policies at Constant VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02775)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AURA-Mem是一种面向机器人长期推理任务的高效内存机制，通过基于动作效用的可学习门控减少不必要的KV写入，在保持性能的同时显著降低内存写入次数和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统KV-cache适用于数据中心批量短请求场景，但在机器人长周期、非重置、资源受限的边缘部署中会导致内存写入过多、资源消耗大等问题，难以适用。

Method: 提出AURA-Mem，结合冻结的视觉-语言-动作主干网络，引入固定大小的循环记忆和可学习门控机制，仅在当前观测会影响下一步动作时才写入记忆；门控直接通过闭环动作误差信号进行训练，而非重构误差。

Result: 在合成基准上，AURA-Mem在匹配最佳O(1)基线准确率的同时，减少5.19-6.13倍写入，最多达9.19倍；在OpenVLA-OFT 7B模型与LIBERO-Long任务上，成功率达0.233，优于始终写入的KV变体（0.217），且写入减少7.0倍，内存恒定。

Conclusion: AURA-Mem通过动作相关性驱动的记忆写入机制，有效适应了具身智能体在边缘设备上的长期推理需求，在写入效率和内存使用上显著优于KV-cache，为资源受限场景提供了更优的记忆架构。

Abstract: The KV-cache is the right memory for datacenters but the wrong memory for robots. Datacenter inference batches many short requests and resets them, amortizing an attention cache across a crowd. Embodied agents instead run one long, non-resetting episode on bandwidth-limited edge hardware, where high-bandwidth memory and flash are scarce, flash has finite write endurance, and memory writes rather than compute can become the binding constraint.
  AURA-Mem (Action-Utility Recurrent Adaptive Memory) targets this regime. It wraps a frozen vision-language-action backbone with a constant-size recurrent memory and a learned gate that writes only when the current observation would change the next action: memory that knows when to stay silent. Unlike reconstruction-based memory, the gate is trained directly against a closed-loop action-error signal. Its inference state is fixed at 4,224 bytes regardless of horizon, while a KV-cache grows to 6,061 times larger at 100,000 steps.
  On a controlled synthetic benchmark, AURA-Mem matches the best O(1) baseline in accuracy while using 5.19-6.13 times fewer writes, and up to 9.19 times fewer writes on easier configurations. Budget-matched random and periodic schedules do not recover this gain, isolating the benefit to the action-surprise signal. On a trained closed-loop OpenVLA-OFT 7B panel on LIBERO-Long (n=60 episodes per arm), the gate does not hurt success: AURA-Mem matches the ungated base policy (0.233) and slightly exceeds an always-write KV arm (0.217), while using 7.0 times fewer writes and constant memory. We also instantiate an approximate-information-state value-loss bound as a methodology demonstration; at this scale, the bound is vacuous rather than a guarantee.

</details>


### [218] [Evaluating Transformer and LSTM Frameworks for Prediction in Ungauged Basins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02791)
*Taye Akinrele,James Halgren,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究比较了LSTM和仅编码器Transformer在无观测流域上游径流推断中的表现，发现LSTM整体性能更优，且下游信息显著提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在水文观测数据有限的情况下，Transformer是否比LSTM更适合用于推断上游径流过程。

Method: 基于NOAA国家水模型（NWM）的回溯模拟数据，比较LSTM与编码器-only Transformer在不同配置下的预测性能。

Result: LSTM在上游-only和联合配置下均优于Transformer；引入下游信息使所有模型中位NNSE提升超60%。

Conclusion: 对于上游径流重建任务，循环记忆结构（如LSTM）比编码器-only Transformer更具优势，下游水文信息可作为强辅助约束显著提升预测能力。

Abstract: Watershed networks exhibit convergent topologies in which multiple tributaries merge into downstream channels,integrating diverse upstream hydrological processes. In ungauged basins, the absence of direct observations increases uncertainty and limits the ability to anticipate extreme events. This study evaluates whether an encoder-only Transformer provides an advantage over an LSTM for upstream streamflow inference under limited hydrologic information, using retrospective simulations from the NOAA National Water Model (NWM). Across both upstream-only and combined configurations, the LSTM showed stronger overall performance than the Transformer model across the two configurations. Incorporating downstream information further boosted performance for all models, increasing median NNSE by more than 60%. Rather than treating this as a leaderboard-style comparison, we interpret the experiments as a test of architectural inductive bias for hydrologic sequence inference. The results indicate that recurrent memory remains better aligned with this upstream reconstruction task than an encoder-only Transformer, while downstream hydrologic context provides a strong auxiliary constraint that substantially improves prediction skill across architectures

</details>


### [219] [BehaviorBench: Modeling Real-World User Decisions from Behavioral Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02798)
*Liangwei Yang,Jielin Qiu,Zixiang Chen,Ming Zhu,Juntao Tan,Zhiwei Liu,Wenting Zhao,Zhujun Lan,Akshara Prabhakar,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang,Shelby Heinecke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BehaviorBench是一个基于真实世界行为痕迹的基准，用于评估个性化决策建模，涵盖信念预测和交易预测两个任务层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户理解基准多依赖模拟用户或模型生成行为，可能与真实人类行为存在系统性偏差，因此需要基于真实行为数据的评估基准。

Method: 构建钱包级别的决策历史，从公开的预测市场和链上记录中提取数据，设计信念预测和交易预测两个任务，并评估不同个性化方法在四种历史接口下的表现。

Result: 在2000个评估钱包中包含大量信念和交易实例，发现个性化对信念预测的提升更稳定，不同任务层和指标下模型排名变化明显，不同历史接口暴露不同失败模式。

Conclusion: BehaviorBench为研究个性化方法是否能有效利用真实世界行为证据提供了新的评估环境，减少了对模拟用户的依赖。

Abstract: Many decision-support settings require systems that adapt to individual users, but evaluation data for this problem remain limited. Existing benchmarks for user understanding often rely on simulated users or model-generated behavior, even though recent work cautions that model-based simulations can diverge systematically from human behavior. We introduce \textsc{BehaviorBench}, a benchmark for evaluating personalized decision modeling from real-world behavioral traces. \textsc{BehaviorBench} reconstructs wallet-level decision histories from observed public prediction-market and on-chain records, and organizes them into two complementary task layers: \emph{Belief prediction}, which predicts a user's final revealed stance and confidence in a market, and \emph{Trade prediction}, which predicts the direction and amount of individual transactions. Across 2,000 evaluation wallets, the benchmark contains 141,445 Belief instances and 1,485,972 Trade instances, with disjoint support pools for retrieval-based evaluation. We evaluate frontier and open-weight generative models under four history interfaces: no personalization, direct recent history, generated user profiles, and retrieved support-wallet evidence. Personalization improves Belief prediction more consistently than Trade prediction, model rankings change across task layers and metrics, and different history interfaces expose different failure modes. \textsc{BehaviorBench} provides an evaluation setting for studying whether personalized methods can use real-world behavioral evidence rather than simulated users alone.

</details>


### [220] [ChatHealthAI: Aligning Electronic Health Record Representations with Large Language Models for Grounded Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02802)
*Bo-Hong Wang,Baicheng Peng,Ruilin Wang,Jun Bai,Ziyang Song,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChatHealthAI 是一个将结构化电子健康记录（EHR）表示与大语言模型（LLM）语义空间对齐的多模态推理框架，通过任务感知采样器实现可解释的临床预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在处理结构化纵向电子健康记录（EHR）方面存在不足，而EHR基础模型虽能学习患者表征却缺乏自然语言推理能力，因此需要结合两者优势以提升临床决策支持系统的性能和可解释性。

Method: 提出ChatHealthAI框架，利用预训练的EHR基础模型提取纵向患者表征，并通过任务感知重采样器将其对齐到冻结的大语言模型的语义空间中，从而实现基于自然语言的临床推理。

Result: 在EHRSHOT基准的三个临床预测任务上评估显示，ChatHealthAI在保持有竞争力的预测性能的同时，显著提升了推理质量和可解释性。

Conclusion: 将EHR基础模型与大语言模型结合可有效支持可解释的临床预测，为未来智能医疗系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong natural-language reasoning abilities for clinical decision support, but struggle to effectively model structured longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs). In contrast, EHR foundation models can learn predictive patient representations, yet lack interpretable language-based reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose ChatHealthAI, a multimodal reasoning framework that aligns structured EHR representations from a pretrained EHR foundation model with the semantic space of a frozen LLM through a task-aware resampler. By integrating longitudinal patient representations with refined clinical event descriptions, ChatHealthAI enables clinically grounded natural-language reasoning while maintaining accurate patient prediction. We evaluated ChatHealthAI on three clinical predictive tasks from the EHRSHOT benchmark. Results show that ChatHealthAI improves reasoning quality and interpretability while preserving competitive predictive performance. These findings highlight the potential of integrating EHR foundation models with pretrained LLMs for interpretable clinical prediction.

</details>


### [221] [Traj-Evolve: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for Patient Trajectory Modeling in Lung Cancer Early Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02812)
*Sihang Zeng,Matthew Thompson,Ruth Etzioni,Meliha Yetisgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Traj-Evolve是一种自进化的多智能体系统，通过经验池和多智能体强化学习建模患者纵向电子健康记录，提升临床预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统在处理患者轨迹时孤立分析个体病例，未能模拟医生借鉴相似先例经验的临床实践过程。

Method: 提出Traj-Evolve，包含两个演化机制：一是构建非参数化的经验池（ExPool），通过检索相似患者的推理轨迹提供少样本上下文；二是采用基于奖励排序微调的多智能体强化学习（MARL）优化智能体间及智能体与记忆间的协作；并通过留一交叉检索策略统一训练与推理行为。

Result: 在利用长达五年多模态EHR数据的肺癌预测任务中，Traj-Evolve在整体人群和从不吸烟人群中均优于9个强基线模型；动态分析表明ExPool使检索从多样转向具体样本，MARL中管理者损失快速收敛而工作者持续受益，且两者在风险预测上互补——ExPool提高特异性，MARL提高敏感性。

Conclusion: Traj-Evolve通过非参数化记忆与参数化学习的协同演化，有效建模长程、稀疏、多模态的患者轨迹，更贴近真实临床决策过程并提升预测表现。

Abstract: Modeling patient trajectories from longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) requires reasoning over sparse, noisy, and long-context multimodal sequences. Existing LLM-based multi-agent systems address context length but process patients in isolation, failing to mirror how clinicians leverage accumulated experience from similar prior cases. We present Traj-Evolve, a self-evolving multi-agent system with two complementary evolving mechanisms. First, an Experience Pool (ExPool) acts as a non-parametric memory, indexing rejection-sampled reasoning traces to retrieve similar patients as few-shot contexts. Second, multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) via reward-ranked fine-tuning parametrically optimizes inter-agent and agent-memory collaboration. A leave-one-out cross-retrieval strategy unifies the two, aligning training- and inference-time behavior under retrieval augmentation. On a lung cancer prediction task utilizing up to five years of multimodal EHRs, Traj-Evolve outperforms 9 strong baselines on the overall population and a challenging never-smoker population. Analysis of the evolving dynamics highlights three key findings: (1) expanding the ExPool shifts optimal retrieval from diverse to specific samples; (2) under MARL, the manager agent's prediction loss converges quickly while the worker agents' temporal reasoning continues to benefit from more verified patients; and (3) the two mechanisms are complementary on the predicted risk, where ExPool improves specificity while MARL improves sensitivity.

</details>


### [222] [An Exploration of Collision-based Enemy Morphology Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02832)
*Johor Jara Gonzalez,Matthew Guzdial*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了三种基于玩家碰撞信息生成游戏敌人形态的新方法，并比较了它们相对于机器人学中进化基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管在程序化内容生成（PCG）方面已有大量研究，但针对生成视频游戏中的敌人形态的研究仍较少，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出了三种新颖的方法来生成敌人的形态，这些方法基于玩家与敌人之间的碰撞信息，并借鉴了机器人学中的相关形态生成研究。

Result: 每种方法各有优劣，但在性能上均达到或超过了从机器人学形态生成工作中改进而来的进化算法基线。

Conclusion: 基于玩家碰撞信息生成敌人形态是可行且有效的，未来可在游戏设计中进一步探索和应用此类方法。

Abstract: Despite a great deal of prior research into Procedural Content Generation (PCG), relatively little prior work has explored generating enemies for video games. In particular, there is almost no work on generating enemy morphologies, the basic body plan or collision information for in-game enemies, despite the existence of related morphology generation work in robotics. In this paper, we explore three different novel approaches to generate enemy morphologies based on player collision information. We found that each approach provides different strengths and weaknesses, but all had equivalent or better performance than an evolutionary baseline adapted from prior robotics morphology work.

</details>


### [223] [Thinking Past the Answer: Evaluating Harmful Overthinking in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02835)
*Simone Caldarella,Davide Talon,Rahaf Aljundi,Elisa Ricci,Massimiliano Mancini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型推理模型在得出正确答案后继续推理的影响，提出了前缀级轨迹评估协议，发现过多的推理可能导致有害的过度思考，从而降低准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型推理模型在达到正确答案后继续推理是否有益，分析过度思考对模型性能的影响。

Method: 引入基于推理充分性的前缀级轨迹评估协议，定义模型首次生成正确答案所需的最小推理预算，以区分无害的冗长过度思考和有害的过度思考。

Result: 实验表明，在多模态和纯语言推理基准上，许多任务实际上需要较少的推理；在首次正确前缀处停止推理可使准确率提高高达21%；常见的效率策略如早停法虽能减少冗长过度思考（最多50%），但无法缓解有害的过度思考；错误分析显示逻辑漂移和视觉重解释是主要原因。

Conclusion: 当前模型不仅受限于推理能力，更受限于无法在合适时机停止推理，有害的过度思考是一个广泛的可靠性风险。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by generating explicit intermediate reasoning traces through increased test-time compute, yet the assumption that longer reasoning is consistently beneficial remains under-examined. While recent evidence shows that additional reasoning can lead models to overthink, we ask: "Once a model has reached the correct answer, does further reasoning refine the solution, or deviate from it?" To study the dynamics after correctness, we introduce a prefix-level trajectory evaluation protocol grounded in reasoning sufficiency, defining the minimum reasoning budget required for a model to first generate the correct answer. This allows us to disentangle verbose overthinking, where additional reasoning is redundant but harmless, from harmful overthinking, where continued reasoning destabilizes an already-correct trajectory. Starting from multimodal benchmarks, we find that many instances considered reasoning-intensive require surprisingly little reasoning. Moreover, stopping at the first correct prefix improves accuracy over standard reasoning up to 21%, revealing that current models are limited not only by their ability to reason, but also by their inability to stop at the right time. Furthermore, while common efficiency strategies like early stopping substantially reduce verbose overthinking (up to 50%), they fail to mitigate harmful overthinking. Failure analysis reveals that correctness deviations are mainly driven by logical drift and visual reinterpretation. Finally, we show that our findings generalize to language-only reasoning benchmarks, highlighting harmful overthinking as a broader reliability risk. Code available at https://simonecaldarella.github.io/thinking-past-the-answer.

</details>


### [224] [Toward a Modular Architecture for Embedded AI Agent Systems at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02862)
*Marcus Rüb,Michael Gerhards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化的嵌入式智能体系统参考架构，通过分层设计将设备端轻量级智能体与云端增强型智能体相结合，并引入治理层以确保安全性、可观测性和策略执行，从而在资源受限的嵌入式环境中实现可靠的自主智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于嵌入式微控制器在内存和能耗方面的严格限制，当前大型语言模型驱动的智能体难以直接部署于普适计算环境中，现有框架多依赖服务器资源或持续联网，缺乏对深度嵌入式系统的支持。

Method: 提出一种分层的模块化架构，分离设备端智能体（运行压缩神经网络和规则逻辑）与云增强智能体（利用小型语言模型进行高层推理），并通过跨层级的治理层实现分布式设备群的安全与策略管控。

Result: 该架构在延迟、能耗和可靠执行之间实现了良好权衡，支持低延迟、隐私敏感任务的本地处理以及复杂任务的云端协同推理，具备在资源受限环境下部署自主智能的能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的参考架构为在资源受限的嵌入式系统中实现安全、可控的智能体AI提供了可行路径，弥合了实时控制与自主智能之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The rise of Large Language Models (LLMs) has enabled agentic AI capable of complex reasoning and tool use; however, deploying such autonomy in pervasive computing environments remains challenging due to the strict memory and energy constraints of embedded microcontrollers. Existing frameworks typically assume server-class resources or continuous connectivity, leaving a gap for deeply embedded systems. This paper proposes a modular reference architecture for Embedded Agent Systems that bridges the divide between deterministic real-time control and agentic intelligence.
  We introduce a tiered design that decouples On-Device Agents - executing highly compressed neural networks and rule-based logic for low-latency, privacy-critical tasks - from Cloud-Augmented Agents that leverage Small Language Models (SLMs) for higher-level reasoning and planning. A key contribution is the integration of a cross-cutting Governance Layer, ensuring observability, policy enforcement, and safety across distributed fleets of autonomous devices. Rather than presenting purely empirical benchmarks, we analyze architectural design principles and trade-offs regarding latency, energy, and reliable execution in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [225] [Don't Gamble, GAMBLe: An Analytical Framework for AI-Driven Research Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02863)
*Marquita Ellis,Paul Castro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GAMBLe框架，用于分析AI驱动的研究系统（ADRS）中各组件的相互作用，揭示了生成器与评估器对优化景观的影响，并通过大量实验表明组件选择对性能和搜索效率有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ADRS系统性能依赖于组件间的复杂交互，但缺乏有效的分析工具，且传统收敛性保证不适用于实际ADRS过程。

Method: 提出GAMBLe框架，将ADRS分解为生成器、评估器、发现机制和预算四个参数，并引入有效景观L_eff = A∘G来刻画每问题的优化结构；在760多次实验中测试不同生成器与机制组合。

Result: 实验显示不存在绝对最优的生成器或机制，某些开源模型可超越前沿模型，最简单机制有时优于先进元搜索；在有限预算下，合理组件选择可提升性能13-67%，搜索效率提高6-39倍。

Conclusion: ADRS的性能高度依赖于组件间的适配关系，GAMBLe框架为理解和优化此类系统提供了新的分析视角与工具。

Abstract: AI-Driven Research Systems (ADRS) -- systems coupling LLMs with automated evaluation to discover algorithms, proofs, and designs -- are being optimized and adopted across domains, but the tools to analyze them have not kept pace.
